# Source owners, active facts, and conditional adoption

The 707 retained settings files and 959 H2/file-level records keep their original source identity. The first column names that retained source owner, not a new active unit. The second names the owner only of facts actually expressed in the current body; it does not activate every detail of the linked source H2. An em dash means that no independent current fact record has been authored for that source subject. The third column is a conditional adoption entrance, never a competing fact owner. Retained constraints and qualifications survive conditional selection; using a new subject or value requires a usable subject H2, an updated active link, and the applicable settings evidence and review before downstream reliance. No row proves fresh historical verification or grants the same evidence to every source record.

## R1 bounded fact-input additions

The retained census remains 707 files. The root's SG01/SG02 fact clearance is an additional bounded input, not a newly read diplomatic primary source: [Egypt purpose](../body/settings/100-history/030-decisions-and-results.md#egypt-purpose) and [Prussian demand](../body/settings/100-history/030-decisions-and-results.md#prussian-demand) own only their literal selected values. The [Jacquard owner](../body/settings/030-society/020-credit-knowledge-and-industry.md#jacquard) gives precedence to the root's bounded venue/mechanism check over the retained workshop shorthand. These private successor owners require separate new-settings evidence and independent review before actual downstream admission. No old proof is renewed.

The root's [durable SG03 note](../references/research/20261026/napoleon-sg03-venice-segregation-source-check-20261026.md) and [residence/daytime context](../references/research/20261026/napoleon-sg03-residence-and-daytime-access-context-20261026.md) additionally support the [Venice gate/segregation owner](../body/settings/030-society/020-credit-knowledge-and-industry.md#venice-gates), limited to 1797 gate removal, temporary end of compelled ghetto residence and the Austrian-return qualification; ordinary daytime exit is not newly granted. This is not public-office eligibility, permanent emancipation or archival verification; the resident's specific choice and speech are authored fiction.

The root’s bounded fact-input records are preserved byte-exact under production-owned research: [SG01/SG02](../references/research/20261026/napoleon-sg01-sg02-political-purpose-research-20261026.md), [Jacquard](../references/research/20261026/napoleon-jacquard-realization-source-check-20261026.md), SG03/residence and bank-scope notes linked above. They preserve their actual reading attribution and limits; no retained source or historical cache note is rewritten.
